           │         which is a real number:                                                                                                                       │

           │         pass                                                                                                                                          │

           │ from enum import Enum                                                                                                                                 │

           │ class TrafficLightState(Enum):                                                                                                                        │
           │     GREEN = 1                                                                                                                                         │
           │     YELLOW = 2                                                                                                                                        │
           │     RED = 3                                                                                                                                           │
           │     AMBER = 4                                                                                                                                         │
           │                                                                                                                                                       │
           │ class TrafficLight:                                                                                                                                   │
           │     def __init__(self):                                                                                                                               │
           │         self.state = TrafficLightState.RED                                                                                                            │
           │                                                                                                                                                       │
           │     def set_state(self, state):                                                                                                                       │
           │         self.state = state                                                                                                                            │
           │                                                                                                                                                       │
           │ class Controller:                                                                                                                                     │
           │     def __init__(self):                                                                                                                               │
           │         self.L1 = TrafficLight()                                                                                                                      │
           │         self.L2 = TrafficLight()                                                                                                                      │
           │         self.L3 = TrafficLight()                                                                                                                      │
           │         self.L4 = TrafficLight()                                                                                                                      │
           │                                                                                                                                                       │
           │         self.sequence =                                                                                                                               │
           │                                                                                                                                                       │
           │     def next(self):                                                                                                                                   │
           │         for light in self.sequence:                                                                                                                   │
           │             if light.state == TrafficLightState.GREEN:                                                                                                │
           │                 light.set_state(TrafficLightState.AMBER)                                                                                              │
           │             elif light.state == TrafficLightState.AMBER:                                                                                              │
           │                 light.set_state(TrafficLightState.RED)                                                                                                │
           │                                                                                                                                                       │
           │         for i in range(len(self.sequence)):                                                                                                           │
           │             if self.sequence.state == TrafficLightState.RED:                                                                                          │
           │                 if i + 1 < len(self.sequence):                                                                                                        │
           │                     self.sequence.set_state(TrafficLightState.GREEN)                                                                                  │
           │                 else:                                                                                                                                 │
           │                     self.sequence[0].set_state(TrafficLightState.GREEN)                                                                               │
           │                                                                                                                                                       │
           │ class Junction(Module):                                                                                                                               │
           │     def specification(self):                                                                                                                          │
           │         return self.L1.state == TrafficLightState.GREEN and self.L3.state == TrafficLightState.GREEN and self.L2.state == TrafficLightState.RED and   │
           │ self.L4.state == TrafficLightState.RED                                                                                                                │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.                                                                                                  │

           │                                                                                                                                                       │
           │     def types(self):                                                                                                                                  │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.                                                                                                  │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a 8-bit type called T:                                                                      │
           │         ```                                                                                                                                           │
           │         def types(self):                                                                                                                              │
           │             self.T = BitVector(8)                                                                                                                     │
           │         ```                                                                                                                                           │
           │         """                                                                                                                                           │
           │         pass                                                                                                                                          │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their types.                                                                                    │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an 8-bit variable x                                                                         │
           │         and an integer variable y:                                                                                                                    │
           │         ```                                                                                                                                           │
           │         def locals(self):                                                                                                                             │
           │             self.x = BitVector(8)                                                                                                                     │
           │             self.y = Integer()                                                                                                                        │
           │         ```                                                                                                                                           │
           │         """                                                                                                                                           │
           │         pass                                                                                                                                          │
           │                                                                                                                                                       │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their types.                                                                                    │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an input variable x,                                                                        │
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by 2-bit bitvectors:                                                                            │

           │         pass                                                                                                                                          │

           │         greater than or equal to 20:                                                                                                                  │
